r\"(\\d{4}[\\.-]\\d{1,2}(?:[\\.-]\\d{1,2})?)\\s*(?:~|–|-|to)\\s*(\\d{4}[\\.-]\\d{1,2}(?:[\\.-]\\d{1,2})?)\",\n        text,\n    )\n    if range_m:\n        s1, s2 = range_m.group(1), str, values: dict[str, object]) -> tuple[str, set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Replace all {{key}} placeholders using values dict.\n\n    Returns (new_text, used_keys)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    used: set[str] = Tag is namespaced; localname check is easiest.\n    for p in root.iter():\n        if localname(p.tag) != \"p\":\n            continue\n\n        # Collect all <hp:t> nodes under this paragraph.\n        t_nodes = [el for el in p.iter() if localname(el.tag) == \"t\"]\n        if not t_nodes:\n            continue\n\n        original_full = paragraph_text(t_nodes)\n        used_keys: set[str] = set()\n\n        #
